If an implementation stores literal cache-key strings, those\nstrings persist to disk; if it stores hashed keys, the hash persists.\n\n- **Never include secrets in cache keys.** Strip `Authorization`, bearer tokens, API\n  keys, tenant tokens, signed metadata, and custom auth headers before building or\n  hashing a key. OpenCodeSessionHandle): void {\n    // Remove oldest sessions if we've hit the limit\n    while (this.sessions.size >= MAX_SESSIONS && this.sessionOrder.length > 0) {\n      const oldestKey =    }\n    }\n    this.sessions.set(cacheKey, session);\n    this.sessionOrder.push(cacheKey);\n  }\n\n  private prepareCall(context?: CallApiContextParams): OpenCodePreparedCall {\n    const config: \"./gen/sdk.gen.js\";\nexport { OpencodeClient };\nfunction pick(value, fallback, encode) {\n    if (!value)\n        return;\n    if (!fallback)\n        return value;\n    if (value === fallback)\n        return fallback;\n    if (encode && value === encode(fallback))\n        return fallback;\n    return value;\n}\nfunction rewrite(request, values) {\n    if (request.method !== \"GET\" && request.method !== \"HEAD\")\n        return request;\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    let changed = false;\n    for (const [name, key] of [\n        [\"x-opencode-directory\", \"directory\"],\n        [\"x-opencode-workspace\", \"workspace\"],\n    ]) {\n        const value = pick(request.headers.get(name), key === \"directory\" ? values.directory : values.workspace, key === \"directory\" ? encodeURIComponent : undefined);\n        if (!value)\n            continue;\n        if (!url.searchParams.has(key)) {\n            url.searchParams.set(key, value);\n        }\n next.headers.delete(\"x-opencode-workspace\");\n    return next;\n}\nexport function createOpencodeClient(config) {\n    if (!config?.fetch) {\n        const customFetch = (req) => {\n            //
